korean_wang_goryeo_1_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:korean_wang_goryeo_1
			exists = character:korean_wang_goryeo_42
		}
		character:korean_wang_goryeo_1 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:korean_wang_goryeo_42 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

			exists = character:korean_du_dureung_11
			exists = character:korean_du_dureung_9
		}
		character:korean_du_dureung_11 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:korean_du_dureung_9 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
